- **primary-grade-grader**: Supports primary school teachers with grade calculations, feedback, rubrics, student data organization, grade recording, trend analysis, individualized criteria, grade communication, moderation, and special-circumstance adjustments. Use when grading assignments, computing weighted grades, drafting feedback or parent messages, building rubrics or grade sheets, or analyzing classroom performance data. ([nexibeo/plugins-completeaitraining.com](https://github.com/nexibeo/plugins-completeaitraining.com/tree/HEAD/completeai-teachers/skills/primary-grade-grader))
- **primary-hardlight-city**: Wibi Style 官方视觉风格：把一张照片转换成原色硬光都市。适合城市街道、建筑、公共交通、旅行纪实和环境人物照片。适用于用户要求原色硬光都市，或明确调用 $primary-hardlight-city 时。 ([Vieeeeeee/wibi-style](https://github.com/Vieeeeeee/wibi-style/tree/HEAD/skills/primary-hardlight-city) · ★188 · 2 copies)
- **primary-id-uniqueness-check**: Use when a freshly uploaded .csv or .xlsx metabolomics table must be validated for threshold-exceeding missing data, non-numeric concentration entries, or a missing/non-unique primary ID before imputation and normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/primary-id-uniqueness-check))
- **primary-key-coverage-identity**: Primary Key Coverage & Identity Columns ([CarlosCaPe/octorato](https://github.com/CarlosCaPe/octorato/tree/HEAD/skills/primary-key-coverage-identity) · ★19)
- **primary-learning-plan-generator**: Merges multiple job-specific gap analyses (from Acme Payroll, Example Finance, Example Consulting, or any other role) into ONE cross-referenced master learning plan, and refreshes an existing master plan against live Pluralsight/MS Learn completion data. Use whenever the user says "merge these gap analyses into one plan", "update the master learning plan", "refresh my learning plan with latest course history", "combine these learning plans", pastes/uploads more than one gap analysis at once, or uploads a previous Master Learning Plan PDF and asks it to be brought current. Do NOT use this for… ([andreak3779/job-o-matic-assistant](https://github.com/andreak3779/job-o-matic-assistant/tree/HEAD/skills/primary_learning_plan_generator) · ★2)
- **primary-literature-confirmation-mapping**: Use when a metabolic network reconstruction (e.g. from KEGG/NCBI-RefSeq mapping in MINNO) has yielded predicted missing reactions and each candidate needs an evidence label — primary-literature confirmation vs homology support — before downstream use. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/primary-literature-confirmation-mapping))
- **primary-logic**: Real-time investment context from Primary Logic — LLM-ranked relevance and impact signals from podcasts, articles, X/Twitter, Kalshi, Polymarket, earnings calls, filings, and other monitored sources across public and private companies. ([PrimaryLogic/agent-skills](https://github.com/PrimaryLogic/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/primary-logic))
- **primary-market-diligence**: Use when Codex needs to run or draft an一级市场 investment research / commercial diligence workflow for a private company, new business direction, industrial project, financing, investment, joint venture, or government-platform cooperation. The skill turns scattered public sources and NDA/internal company materials into industry analysis, company business analysis, company-vs-industry strengths and risks, investment plan, and risk-control measures. ([WHUEugene/diligence-skills](https://github.com/WHUEugene/diligence-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/primary-market-diligence) · ★1)
- **primary-market-diligence-planner**: Build a focused diligence workplan for Chinese primary-market technology investments, including issue trees, material request lists, management interview agendas, expert-call questions, competitor checks, customer validation plans, and next-step priorities. Use when the user is preparing diligence for a startup, hard-tech company, quantum/AI/semiconductor/new-energy project, or any private-market deal and needs a practical DD plan rather than an investment conclusion. ([Jackhammer1024/Skills-for-Primary-Market-](https://github.com/Jackhammer1024/Skills-for-Primary-Market-/tree/HEAD/primary-market-diligence-planner))
- **primary-market-project-judgment**: Structure Chinese primary-market technology investment judgment for startups, venture deals, private-market projects, pitch decks, research notes, interviews, or sector theses. Use when the user wants an investment view based on demand, value-chain position, competitive variables, company positioning, cycle stage, commercialization certainty, risks, valuation, and final IC-ready conclusion. ([Jackhammer1024/Skills-for-Primary-Market-](https://github.com/Jackhammer1024/Skills-for-Primary-Market-/tree/HEAD/primary-market-project-judgment))
- **primary-market-research**: 生成专业的一级市场/风险投资（PE/VC）行业研究报告，覆盖TMT、消费、医疗健康、工业等常见赛道，输出深度讨论型（15-25页）或传统行业型（30-60页）的PDF、DOCX或PPTX文档。当用户要求创建行业研究报告、赛道深度研究、市场分析报告、投资备忘录、Best Ideas摘要，或请求分析特定行业、市场趋势、竞争格局及技术图谱时触发。 ([haomingz/kimi-skills](https://github.com/haomingz/kimi-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/primary-market-research) · ★25 · 5 copies)
- **primary-math-bs-textbook-sync**: 把北师大版小学数学的年级、册别、单元和学习场景，转成预习、同步练习、错题追练、单元复习和考前巩固等可直接执行的学习任务。把教材单元变成今天能做、明天能复盘、错了能追练的学习闭环。 Workflow: primary_math_bs_textbook_sync.run. - **primary-math-mental-arithmetic**: 为小学生生成短、准、可批改的口算训练，让每天 5-10 分钟练出速度、准确率和数感。 Workflow: primary_math_mental_arithmetic.run. ([hezkvectory/hermes-edu-skills](https://github.com/hezkvectory/hermes-edu-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/daily-practice/primary-math-mental-arithmetic) · ★147 · 2 copies)
- **primary-math-question-generator**: 小学数学试题仿写专家，能够分析小学数学题目的知识领域、核心概念、数学思想方法、难度等级和认知要求，并生成等价替代试题和多种变式题目 ([sunqb/ccsdk](https://github.com/sunqb/ccsdk/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/primary-math-question-generator) · ★4)
